[tool-e33] read_file ->    562	static int no_orphaned_work (void)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    563	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    564	  return
[tool-e33] read_file ->    565	    atomic_load_acquire(&orph_structs.ephe_list_live) == 0 &&
[tool-e33] read_file ->    566	    atomic_load_acquire(&orph_structs.final_info) == NULL;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    567	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    568	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    569	static void adopt_orphaned_work (void)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    570	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    571	  caml_domain_state* domain_state = Caml_state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    572	  value orph_ephe_list_live, last;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    573	  struct caml_final_info *f, *myf, *temp;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    574	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    575	  if (no_orphaned_work() || caml_domain_is_terminating())
[tool-e33] read_file ->    576	    return;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    577	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    578	  caml_plat_lock_blocking(&orphaned_lock);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    579	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    580	  orph_ephe_list_live = orph_structs.ephe_list_live;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    581	  orph_structs.ephe_list_live = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    582	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    583	  f = orph_structs.final_info;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    584	  orph_structs.final_info = NULL;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    585	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    586	  caml_plat_unlock(&orphaned_lock);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    587	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    588	  if (orph_ephe_list_live) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    589	    last = ephe_list_tail(orph_ephe_list_live);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    590	    CAMLassert(Ephe_link(last) == 0);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    591	    Ephe_link(last) = domain_state->ephe_info->live;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    592	    domain_state->ephe_info->live = orph_ephe_list_live;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    593	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    594	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    595	  while (f != NULL) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    596	    myf = domain_state->final_info;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    597	    CAMLassert (caml_gc_phase == Phase_sweep_and_mark_main);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    598	    /* Since we are in [Phase_sweep_and_mark_main], the current domain has not
[tool-e33] read_file ->    599	       updated its finalisers. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    600	    CAMLassert (!myf->updated_first);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    601	    CAMLassert (!myf->updated_last);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    602	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    603	    if (f->todo_head) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    604	      /* Adopt the finalising set. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    605	      if (myf->todo_tail == NULL) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    606	        CAMLassert(myf->todo_head == NULL);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    607	        myf->todo_head = f->todo_head;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    608	        myf->todo_tail = f->todo_tail;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    609	      } else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    610	        myf->todo_tail->next = f->todo_head;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    611	        myf->todo_tail = f->todo_tail;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    612	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    613	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    614	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    615	    /* Adopt the finalisable set */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    616	    if (f->first.young > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    617	      caml_final_merge_finalisable (&f->first, &myf->first);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    618	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    619	    if (f->last.young > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    620	      caml_final_merge_finalisable (&f->last, &myf->last);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    621	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    622	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    623	    temp = f;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    624	    f = f->next;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    625	    caml_stat_free (temp);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    626	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    627	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    628	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    629	/*******************************************************************************
[tool-e33] read_file ->    630	 * Pacing
[tool-e33] read_file ->    631	 ******************************************************************************/
[tool-e33] read_file ->    632	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    633	/* These two counters keep track of how much work the GC is supposed to
[tool-e33] read_file ->    634	   do in order to keep up with allocation. Both are in GC work units.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    635	   `alloc_counter` increases when we allocate: the number of words allocated
[tool-e33] read_file ->    636	   is converted to GC work units and added to this counter.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    637	   `work_counter` increases when the GC has done some work.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    638	   The difference between the two is how much the GC is lagging behind
[tool-e33] read_file ->    639	   (or in advance of) allocations.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    640	   These counters can wrap around (see function `diffmod`) as long as they
[tool-e33] read_file ->    641	   don't get too far apart, which is guaranteed by the limited size of
[tool-e33] read_file ->    642	   memory.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    643	*/
[tool-e33] read_file ->    644	static atomic_uintnat alloc_counter;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    645	static atomic_uintnat work_counter;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    646	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    647	static inline intnat max2 (intnat a, intnat b)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    648	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    649	  if (a > b){
[tool-e33] read_file ->    650	    return a;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    651	  }else{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    652	    return b;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    653	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    654	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    655	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    656	static inline intnat min2 (intnat a, intnat b)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    657	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    658	  if (a < b){
[tool-e33] read_file ->    659	    return a;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    660	  }else{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    661	    return b;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    662	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    663	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    664	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    665	static inline intnat max3(intnat a, intnat b, intnat c)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    666	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    667	  if (a > b){
[tool-e33] read_file ->    668	    return max2 (a, c);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    669	  }else{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    670	    return max2 (b, c);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    671	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    672	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    673	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    674	/* Take two natural numbers n1 and n2 and let N = 2^{64}.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    675	   Assume that n1 and n2 are not too far apart (less than N/2).
[tool-e33] read_file ->    676	   Given unsigned numbers x1 = n1 modulo N and x2 = n2 modulo N, return
[tool-e33] read_file ->    677	   the (signed) difference between n1 and n2.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    678	*/
[tool-e33] read_file ->    679	static inline intnat diffmod (uintnat x1, uintnat x2)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    680	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    681	  return (intnat) (x1 - x2);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    682	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    683	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    684	/* Reset the work and alloc counters to be equal to each other, by
[tool-e33] read_file ->    685	 * setting them both equal to the "larger" (in the wrapping-around
[tool-e33] read_file ->    686	 * sense we are using here for work_counter and alloc_counter).
[tool-e33] read_file ->    687	 *
[tool-e33] read_file ->    688	 * For use at times when we have disturbed the major GC from its usual
[tool-e33] read_file ->    689	 * pacing and tempo, for example, after any synchronous major
[tool-e33] read_file ->    690	 * collection.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    691	 */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    692	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    693	void caml_reset_major_pacing(void)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    694	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    695	  bool res;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    696	  do {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    697	    uintnat alloc = atomic_load(&alloc_counter);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    698	    uintnat work = atomic_load(&work_counter);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    699	    uintnat target = alloc;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    700	    if (diffmod(work, alloc) > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    701	      target = work;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    702	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    703	    res = (atomic_compare_exchange_strong(&alloc_counter, &alloc, target) &&
[tool-e33] read_file ->    704	           atomic_compare_exchange_strong(&work_counter, &work, target));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    705	  } while (!res);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    706	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    707	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    708	/* The [log_events] parameter is used to disable writing to the ring for two
[tool-e33] read_file ->    709	   reasons:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    710	   1. To prevent spamming the ring with numerous events generated during
[tool-e33] read_file ->    711	      an opportunistic GC slice.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    712	   2. To avoid logging events when the calling domain is not part of the
[tool-e33] read_file ->    713	      Stop-The-World (STW) participant set. If the domain is not part of
[tool-e33] read_file ->    714	      the STW set, the ring could be torn down concurrently while this domain
[tool-e33] read_file ->    715	      attempts to write to it. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    716	static void
[tool-e33] read_file ->    717	update_major_slice_work(intnat howmuch,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    718	                        int may_access_gc_phase,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    719	                        int log_events /* log events to the ring? */)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    720	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    721	  intnat alloc_work, dependent_work, extra_work, new_work;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    722	  intnat my_alloc_count, my_alloc_direct_count, my_dependent_count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    723	  intnat my_alloc_suspended_count, my_alloc_resumed_count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    724	  double my_extra_count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    725	  caml_domain_state *dom_st = Caml_state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    726	  uintnat heap_words, heap_size, heap_sweep_words, total_cycle_work;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    727	  uintnat percent_free;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    728	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    729	  my_alloc_count = dom_st->allocated_words;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    730	  my_alloc_direct_count = dom_st->allocated_words_direct;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    731	  my_alloc_suspended_count = dom_st->allocated_words_suspended;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    732	  my_alloc_resumed_count = dom_st->allocated_words_resumed;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    733	  my_dependent_count = dom_st->dependent_allocated;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    734	  my_extra_count = dom_st->extra_heap_resources;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    735	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    736	  dom_st->stat_major_words += dom_st->allocated_words;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    737	  dom_st->current_ramp_up_allocated_words_diff +=
[tool-e33] read_file ->    738	    dom_st->allocated_words_suspended;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    739	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    740	  dom_st->allocated_words = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    741	  dom_st->allocated_words_direct = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    742	  dom_st->allocated_words_suspended = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    743	  dom_st->allocated_words_resumed = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    744	  dom_st->dependent_allocated = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    745	  dom_st->extra_heap_resources = 0.0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    746	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    747	  /*
[tool-e33] read_file ->    748	     Free memory at the start of the GC cycle (garbage + free list) (assumed):
[tool-e33] read_file ->    749	                 FM = heap_words * caml_percent_free
[tool-e33] read_file ->    750	                      / (100 + caml_percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    751	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    752	     Assuming steady state and enforcing a constant allocation rate, then
[tool-e33] read_file ->    753	     FM is divided in 2/3 for garbage and 1/3 for free list.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    754	              G = 2 * FM / 3
[tool-e33] read_file ->    755	     G is also the amount of memory that will be used during this cycle
[tool-e33] read_file ->    756	     (still assuming steady state).
[tool-e33] read_file ->    757	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    758	     Proportion of G consumed since the previous slice:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    759	              PH = dom_st->allocated_words / G
[tool-e33] read_file ->    760	                = dom_st->allocated_words * 3 * (100 + caml_percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    761	                  / (2 * heap_words * caml_percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    762	     Proportion of extra-heap resources consumed since the previous slice:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    763	              PE = dom_st->extra_heap_resources
[tool-e33] read_file ->    764	     Proportion of total work to do in this slice:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    765	              P  = max (PH, PE)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    766	     Amount of marking work for the GC cycle:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    767	              MW = heap_words * 100 / (100 + caml_percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    768	     Amount of sweeping work for the GC cycle:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    769	              SW = heap_sweep_words
[tool-e33] read_file ->    770	     Amount of total work for the GC cycle:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    771	              TW = MW + SW
[tool-e33] read_file ->    772	              = heap_words * 100 / (100 + caml_percent_free) + heap_sweep_words
[tool-e33] read_file ->    773	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    774	     Amount of time to spend on this slice:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    775	                 T = P * TT
[tool-e33] read_file ->    776	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    777	     Since we must do TW amount of work in TT time, the amount of work done
[tool-e33] read_file ->    778	     for this slice is:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    779	                 S = P * TW
[tool-e33] read_file ->    780	  */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    781	  heap_size = caml_heap_size(dom_st->shared_heap);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    782	  heap_words = Wsize_bsize(heap_size);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    783	  heap_sweep_words = heap_words;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    784	  percent_free = atomic_load(&caml_percent_free);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    785	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    786	  total_cycle_work =
[tool-e33] read_file ->    787	    heap_sweep_words
[tool-e33] read_file ->    788	    + (uintnat) ((double) heap_words * 100.0 / (100.0 + percent_free));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    789	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    790	  if (heap_words > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    791	    double alloc_ratio =
[tool-e33] read_file ->    792	      total_cycle_work
[tool-e33] read_file ->    793	      * 3.0 * (100 + percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    794	      / heap_words / percent_free / 2.0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    795	    intnat current_alloc_count =
[tool-e33] read_file ->    796	      my_alloc_count - my_alloc_suspended_count + my_alloc_resumed_count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    797	    CAMLassert (current_alloc_count >= 0);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    798	    alloc_work = (intnat) (current_alloc_count * alloc_ratio);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    799	  } else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    800	    alloc_work = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    801	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    802	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    803	  if (dom_st->dependent_size > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    804	    double dependent_ratio =
[tool-e33] read_file ->    805	      total_cycle_work
[tool-e33] read_file ->    806	      * (100 + percent_free)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    807	        / (double)dom_st->dependent_size / (double)percent_free;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    808	    dependent_work = (intnat) (my_dependent_count * dependent_ratio);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    809	  }else{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    810	    dependent_work = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    811	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    812	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    813	  extra_work = (intnat) (my_extra_count * (double) total_cycle_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    814	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    815	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "heap_words = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    816	                  heap_words);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    817	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "allocated_words = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    818	                   my_alloc_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    819	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "allocated_words_direct = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    820	                   my_alloc_direct_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    821	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "allocated_words_suspended = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    822	                   my_alloc_suspended_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    823	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "allocated_words_resumed = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    824	                   my_alloc_resumed_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    825	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "alloc work-to-do = %" CAML_PRIdNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    826	                   alloc_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    827	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "dependent_words = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    828	                   my_dependent_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    829	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "dependent work-to-do = %" CAML_PRIdNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    830	                  dependent_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    831	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "extra_heap_resources = %" CAML_PRIuNAT "u\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    832	                  (uintnat) (my_extra_count * 1000000));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    833	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "extra work-to-do = %" CAML_PRIdNAT "\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    834	                  extra_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    835	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    836	  new_work = max3 (alloc_work, dependent_work, extra_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    837	  atomic_fetch_add (&work_counter, dom_st->major_work_done_between_slices);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    838	  dom_st->major_work_done_between_slices = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    839	  atomic_fetch_add (&alloc_counter, new_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    840	  if (howmuch == AUTO_TRIGGERED_MAJOR_SLICE ||
[tool-e33] read_file ->    841	      howmuch == GC_CALCULATE_MAJOR_SLICE) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    842	    dom_st->slice_target = atomic_load (&alloc_counter);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    843	    dom_st->slice_budget = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    844	  }else{
[tool-e33] read_file ->    845	    /* forced or opportunistic GC slice with explicit quantity */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    846	    dom_st->slice_target = atomic_load (&work_counter);  /* already reached */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    847	    dom_st->slice_budget = howmuch;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    848	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    849	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    850	  caml_gc_log("Updated major work: [%c] "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    851	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " heap_words, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    852	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " allocated, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    853	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " allocated (direct), "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    854	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " allocated (suspended), "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    855	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " allocated (resumed), "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    856	              " %" CAML_PRIdNAT " alloc_work, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    857	              " %" CAML_PRIdNAT " dependent_work, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    858	              " %" CAML_PRIdNAT " extra_work, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    859	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " work counter %s, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    860	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " alloc counter, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    861	              " %" CAML_PRIuNAT " slice target, "
[tool-e33] read_file ->    862	              " %" CAML_PRIdNAT " slice budget"
[tool-e33] read_file ->    863	              ,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    864	              caml_gc_phase_char(may_access_gc_phase),
[tool-e33] read_file ->    865	              heap_words, my_alloc_count, my_alloc_direct_count,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    866	              my_alloc_suspended_count, my_alloc_resumed_count,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    867	              alloc_work, dependent_work, extra_work,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    868	              atomic_load (&work_counter),
[tool-e33] read_file ->    869	              atomic_load (&work_counter) > atomic_load (&alloc_counter)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    870	                ? "[ahead]" : "[behind]",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    871	              atomic_load (&alloc_counter),
[tool-e33] read_file ->    872	              dom_st->slice_target, dom_st->slice_budget
[tool-e33] read_file ->    873	              );
[tool-e33] read_file ->    874	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    875	  if (log_events) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    876	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_HEAP_WORDS, (uintnat)heap_words);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    877	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_ALLOCATED_WORDS, my_alloc_count);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    878	    /* TODO: add counters for direct, suspended, resumed allocs. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    879	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_ALLOCATED_WORK, alloc_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    880	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_DEPENDENT_WORK, dependent_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    881	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_EXTRA_WORK, extra_work);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    882	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_WORK_COUNTER, atomic_load (&work_counter));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    883	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_ALLOC_COUNTER, atomic_load (&alloc_counter));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    884	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_SLICE_TARGET, dom_st->slice_target);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    885	    CAML_EV_COUNTER(EV_C_MAJOR_SLICE_BUDGET, dom_st->slice_budget);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    886	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    887	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    888	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    889	#define Chunk_size 0x4000
[tool-e33] read_file ->    890	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    891	typedef enum {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    892	  Slice_uninterruptible,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    893	  Slice_interruptible,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    894	  Slice_opportunistic
[tool-e33] read_file ->    895	} collection_slice_mode;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    896	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    897	static intnat get_major_slice_work(collection_slice_mode mode){
[tool-e33] read_file ->    898	  caml_domain_state *dom_st = Caml_state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    899	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    900	  if (mode == Slice_interruptible && caml_incoming_interrupts_queued())
[tool-e33] read_file ->    901	    return 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    902	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    903	  /* calculate how much work remains to do for this slice */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    904	  intnat budget =
[tool-e33] read_file ->    905	    max2 (diffmod (dom_st->slice_target, atomic_load (&work_counter)),
[tool-e33] read_file ->    906	          dom_st->slice_budget);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    907	  return min2(budget, Chunk_size);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    908	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    909	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    910	/* Register the work done by a chunk of slice.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    911	   Clear requested_global_major_slice if the work counter has caught up with
[tool-e33] read_file ->    912	   the slice's target counter. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    913	static void commit_major_slice_work(intnat words_done) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    914	  caml_domain_state *dom_st = Caml_state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    915	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    916	  caml_gc_log ("Commit major slice work:  %" CAML_PRIdNAT " words_done, ",
[tool-e33] read_file ->    917	               words_done);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    918	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    919	  dom_st->slice_budget -= words_done;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    920	  atomic_fetch_add (&work_counter, words_done);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    921	  if (diffmod (dom_st->slice_target, atomic_load (&work_counter)) <= 0){
[tool-e33] read_file ->    922	    /* We've done enough work by ourselves, no need to interrupt the other
[tool-e33] read_file ->    923	       domains. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    924	    dom_st->requested_global_major_slice = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    925	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->    926	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    927	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    928	/*******************************************************************************
[tool-e33] read_file ->    929	 * Marking
[tool-e33] read_file ->    930	 ******************************************************************************/
[tool-e33] read_file ->    931	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    932	/* NB the MARK_STACK_INIT_SIZE must be larger than the number of objects
[tool-e33] read_file ->    933	   that can be in a pool, see POOL_WSIZE */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    934	#define MARK_STACK_INIT_SIZE (1 << 12)
[tool-e33] read_file ->    935	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    936	/* The mark stack consists of two parts:
[tool-e33] read_file ->    937	   1. the stack - a dynamic array of spans of fields that need to be marked, and
[tool-e33] read_file ->    938	   2. the compressed stack - a bitset of fields that need to be marked.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    939	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    940	   The stack is bounded relative to the heap size. When the stack
[tool-e33] read_file ->    941	   overflows the bound, then entries from the stack are compressed and
[tool-e33] read_file ->    942	   transferred into the compressed stack, expect for "large" entries,
[tool-e33] read_file ->    943	   spans of more than BITS_PER_WORD entries, that are more compactly
[tool-e33] read_file ->    944	   represented as spans and remain on the uncompressed stack.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    945	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    946	   When the stack is empty, the compressed stack is processed.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    947	   The compressed stack iterator marks the point up to which
[tool-e33] read_file ->    948	   compressed stack entries have already been processed.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    949	*/
[tool-e33] read_file ->    950	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    951	typedef struct {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    952	  value_ptr start;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    953	  value_ptr end;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    954	} mark_entry; /* represents fields in the span [start, end) */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    955	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    956	struct mark_stack {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    957	  mark_entry* stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    958	  uintnat count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    959	  uintnat size;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    960	  struct addrmap compressed_stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    961	  addrmap_iterator compressed_stack_iter;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    962	};
[tool-e33] read_file ->    963	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    964	#ifdef DEBUG
[tool-e33] read_file ->    965	#define Is_markable(v) \
[tool-e33] read_file ->    966	    (CAMLassert (v != Debug_free_major), \
[tool-e33] read_file ->    967	     Is_block(v) && !Is_young(v))
[tool-e33] read_file ->    968	#else
[tool-e33] read_file ->    969	#define Is_markable(v) (Is_block(v) && !Is_young(v))
[tool-e33] read_file ->    970	#endif
[tool-e33] read_file ->    971	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    972	/* Compressed mark stack
[tool-e33] read_file ->    973	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    974	   We use a bitset, implemented as a hashtable storing word-sized
[tool-e33] read_file ->    975	   integers (uintnat). Each integer represents a "chunk" of addresses
[tool-e33] read_file ->    976	   that may or may not be present in the stack.
[tool-e33] read_file ->    977	 */
[tool-e33] read_file ->    978	static const uintnat chunk_mask = ~(uintnat)(BITS_PER_WORD-1);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    979	static inline uintnat ptr_to_chunk(value_ptr ptr) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    980	  return ((uintnat)(ptr) / sizeof(value)) & chunk_mask;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    981	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    982	static inline uintnat ptr_to_chunk_offset(value_ptr ptr) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    983	  return ((uintnat)(ptr) / sizeof(value)) & ~chunk_mask;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    984	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    985	static inline value_ptr chunk_and_offset_to_ptr(uintnat chunk, uintnat offset) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    986	  return (value_ptr)((chunk + offset) * sizeof(value));
[tool-e33] read_file ->    987	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->    988	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    989	Caml_inline int add_addr(struct addrmap* amap, value_ptr ptr) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    990	  uintnat chunk = ptr_to_chunk(ptr);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    991	  uintnat offset = ptr_to_chunk_offset(ptr);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    992	  uintnat flag = (uintnat)1 << offset;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    993	  int new_entry = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    994	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    995	  value* amap_pos = caml_addrmap_insert_pos(amap, chunk);
[tool-e33] read_file ->    996	
[tool-e33] read_file ->    997	  if (*amap_pos == ADDRMAP_NOT_PRESENT) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->    998	    new_entry = 1;
[tool-e33] read_file ->    999	    *amap_pos = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1000	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1001	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1002	  CAMLassert(ptr == chunk_and_offset_to_ptr(chunk, offset));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1003	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1004	  if (!(*amap_pos & flag)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1005	    *amap_pos |= flag;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1006	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1007	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1008	  return new_entry;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1009	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1010	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1011	static void mark_stack_prune(struct mark_stack* stk)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1012	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1013	  /* Since addrmap is (currently) using open address hashing, we cannot insert
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1014	     new compressed stack entries into an existing, partially-processed
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1015	     compressed stack. Thus, we create a new compressed stack and insert the
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1016	     unprocessed entries of the existing compressed stack into the new one. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1017	  uintnat old_compressed_entries = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1018	  struct addrmap new_compressed_stack = ADDRMAP_INIT;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1019	  for (addrmap_iterator it = stk->compressed_stack_iter;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1020	       caml_addrmap_iter_ok(&stk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1021	       it = caml_addrmap_next(&stk->compressed_stack, it)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1022	    value k = caml_addrmap_iter_key(&stk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1023	    value v = caml_addrmap_iter_value(&stk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1024	    caml_addrmap_insert(&new_compressed_stack, k, v);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1025	    ++old_compressed_entries;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1026	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1027	  if (old_compressed_entries > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1028	    caml_gc_log("Preserved %" CAML_PRIdNAT " compressed entries",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1029	                old_compressed_entries);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1030	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1031	  caml_addrmap_clear(&stk->compressed_stack);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1032	  stk->compressed_stack = new_compressed_stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1033	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1034	  /* scan mark stack and compress entries */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1035	  uintnat new_stk_count = 0, compressed_entries = 0, total_words = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1036	  for (uintnat i = 0; i < stk->count; i++) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1037	    mark_entry me = stk->stack[i];
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1038	    total_words += me.end - me.start;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1039	    if (me.end - me.start > BITS_PER_WORD) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1040	      /* keep entry in the stack as more efficient and move to front */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1041	      stk->stack[new_stk_count++] = me;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1042	    } else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1043	      while(me.start < me.end) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1044	        compressed_entries += add_addr(&stk->compressed_stack,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1045	                                       me.start);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1046	        me.start++;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1047	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1048	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1049	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1050	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1051	  caml_gc_log("Compressed %" CAML_PRIdNAT " mark stack words into "
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1052	              "%" CAML_PRIdNAT " mark stack entries and "
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1053	              "%" CAML_PRIdNAT " compressed entries",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1054	              total_words, new_stk_count,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1055	              compressed_entries+old_compressed_entries);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1056	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1057	  stk->count = new_stk_count;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1058	  CAMLassert(stk->count < stk->size);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1059	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1060	  /* setup the compressed stack iterator */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1061	  stk->compressed_stack_iter = caml_addrmap_iterator(&stk->compressed_stack);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1062	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1063	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1064	static void realloc_mark_stack (struct mark_stack* stk)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1065	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1066	  mark_entry* new;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1067	  uintnat mark_stack_large_bsize = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1068	  uintnat mark_stack_bsize = stk->size * sizeof(mark_entry);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1069	  uintnat local_heap_bsize = caml_heap_size(Caml_state->shared_heap);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1070	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1071	  /* When the mark stack might not increase, we count the large mark entries
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1072	     to adjust our alloaction. This is needed because large mark stack entries
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1073	     will not compress and because we are using a domain local heap bound we
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1074	     need to fit large blocks into the local mark stack. See PR#11284 */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1075	  if (mark_stack_bsize >= local_heap_bsize / 32) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1076	    for (uintnat i = 0; i < stk->count; ++i) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1077	      mark_entry* me = &stk->stack[i];
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1078	      if (me->end - me->start > BITS_PER_WORD)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1079	        mark_stack_large_bsize += sizeof(mark_entry);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1080	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1081	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1082	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1083	  if (mark_stack_bsize - mark_stack_large_bsize < local_heap_bsize / 32) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1084	    uintnat target_bsize = (mark_stack_bsize - mark_stack_large_bsize) * 2
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1085	                              + mark_stack_large_bsize;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1086	    caml_gc_log ("Growing mark stack to %" CAML_PRIuNAT "k bytes"
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1087	                 "(large block %" CAML_PRIuNAT "k bytes)\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1088	                 target_bsize / 1024, mark_stack_large_bsize / 1024);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1089	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1090	    new = (mark_entry*) caml_stat_resize_noexc ((char*) stk->stack,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1091	                                                target_bsize);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1092	    if (new != NULL) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1093	      stk->stack = new;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1094	      stk->size = target_bsize / sizeof(mark_entry);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1095	      return;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1096	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1097	    caml_gc_log ("No room for growing mark stack. Compressing..\n");
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1098	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1099	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1100	  caml_gc_log ("Mark stack size is %" CAML_PRIuNAT " bytes (> major heap size "
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1101	               "of this domain %" CAML_PRIuNAT " bytes / 32). Compressing...\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1102	               mark_stack_bsize,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1103	               local_heap_bsize);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1104	  mark_stack_prune(stk);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1105	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1106	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1107	/* This function is used for reads that may race with a concurrent `caml_modify`
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1108	   from the mutator. Without this, TSan would flag it as a race (see section
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1109	   3.2 of comment in tsan.c); however, we have decided that these races are
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1110	   benign. We therefore use this function instead, ensuring that the read is
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1111	   not seen by TSan. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1112	static CAMLno_tsan
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1113	#if defined(WITH_THREAD_SANITIZER)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1114	Caml_noinline
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1115	#endif
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1116	value volatile_load_uninstrumented(volatile value* p) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1117	  return *p;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1118	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1119	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1120	Caml_inline void mark_stack_push_range(struct mark_stack* stk,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1121	                                       value_ptr start, value_ptr end)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1122	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1123	  mark_entry* me;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1124	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1125	  if (stk->count == stk->size)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1126	    realloc_mark_stack(stk);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1127	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1128	  me = &stk->stack[stk->count++];
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1129	  me->start = start;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1130	  me->end = end;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1131	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1132	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1133	/* returns the work done by skipping unmarkable objects */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1134	static intnat mark_stack_push_block(struct mark_stack* stk, value block)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1135	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1136	  int i, end;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1137	  uintnat block_wsz = Wosize_val(block), offset = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1138	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1139	  if (Tag_val(block) == Closure_tag) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1140	    /* Skip the code pointers and integers at beginning of closure;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1141	       start scanning at the first word of the environment part. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1142	    offset = Start_env_closinfo(Closinfo_val(block));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1143	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1144	    CAMLassert(offset <= Wosize_val(block)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1145	      && offset >= Start_env_closinfo(Closinfo_val(block)));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1146	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1147	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1148	  CAMLassert(Has_status_val(block, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1149	  CAMLassert(Is_block(block));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1150	  CAMLassert(!Is_young(block));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1151	  CAMLassert(Tag_val(block) != Infix_tag);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1152	  CAMLassert(Tag_val(block) < No_scan_tag);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1153	  CAMLassert(Tag_val(block) != Cont_tag);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1154	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1155	  /* Optimisation to avoid pushing small, unmarkable objects such as
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1156	     [Some 42] into the mark stack. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1157	  end = (block_wsz < 8 ? block_wsz : 8);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1158	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1159	  for (i = offset; i < end; i++) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1160	    value v = volatile_load_uninstrumented(&Field(block, i));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1161	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1162	    if (Is_markable(v))
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1163	      break;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1164	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1165	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1166	  if (i == block_wsz){
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1167	    /* nothing left to mark and credit header */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1168	    return Whsize_wosize(block_wsz - offset);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1169	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1170	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1171	  mark_stack_push_range(stk,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1172	                        Op_val(block) + i,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1173	                        Op_val(block) + block_wsz);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1174	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1175	  /* take credit for the work we skipped due to the optimisation.
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1176	     we will take credit for the header later as part of marking. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1177	  return i - offset;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1178	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1179	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1180	/* This function shrinks the mark stack back to the MARK_STACK_INIT_SIZE size
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1181	   and is called at domain termination via caml_finish_marking. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1182	void caml_shrink_mark_stack (void)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1183	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1184	  struct mark_stack* stk = Caml_state->mark_stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1185	  intnat init_stack_bsize = MARK_STACK_INIT_SIZE * sizeof(mark_entry);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1186	  mark_entry* shrunk_stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1187	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1188	  caml_gc_log ("Shrinking mark stack to %" CAML_PRIuNAT "k bytes\n",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1189	               init_stack_bsize / 1024);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1190	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1191	  shrunk_stack = (mark_entry*) caml_stat_resize_noexc ((char*) stk->stack,

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1252	                                            intnat budget) {

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1257	     so that they can be stored in registers */

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1259	  uintnat blocks_marked = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1260	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1261	  while (1) {

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1265	      CAMLassert(Is_markable(block));

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1268	       * performance reasons.

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1277	        block -= Infix_offset_hd(hd);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1278	        hd = Hd_val(block);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1279	      }

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1285	      }

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1290	        budget -= Wosize_hd(hd);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1291	        continue;

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1294	again:
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1295	      if (Tag_hd(hd) == Lazy_tag || Tag_hd(hd) == Forcing_tag) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1296	        if (!atomic_compare_exchange_strong(Hp_atomic_val(block), &hd,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1297	              With_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED))) {

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1303	            Hp_atomic_val(block),
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1304	            With_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1305	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1306	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1307	      budget--; /* header word */

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1309	        /* Nothing to scan here */

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1318	        uintnat env_offset = Start_env_closinfo(Closinfo_val(block));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1319	        budget -= env_offset;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1320	        me.start += env_offset;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1321	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1322	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1323	    else if (budget <= 0 || stk->count == 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1324	      if (pb.waterline > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1325	        /* Dequeue from pb even when close to empty, because
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1326	           we have nothing else to do */

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1328	        continue;

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1330	      else {

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1332	           so there's nothing to do */

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1334	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1335	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1336	    else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1337	      me = stk->stack[--stk->count];
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1338	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1339	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1340	    value_ptr scan_end = me.end;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1341	    if (scan_end - me.start > budget) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1342	      intnat scan_len = budget < 0 ? 0 : budget;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1343	      scan_end = me.start + scan_len;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1344	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1345	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1346	    for (; me.start < scan_end; me.start++) {

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1350	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1351	      budget--;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1352	      if (Is_markable(child)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1353	        if (pb_full(&pb))
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1354	          break;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1355	        prefetch_block(child);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1356	        pb_push(&pb, child);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1357	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1358	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1359	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1360	    if (me.start < me.end) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1361	      /* Didn't finish scanning this object, either because budget <= 0,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1362	         or the prefetch buffer filled up. Leave the rest on the stack. */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1363	      mark_stack_push_range(stk, me.start, me.end);

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1365

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1369	        pb_fill_mode(&pb);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1370	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1371	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1372	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1373	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1374	  Caml_state->stat_blocks_marked += blocks_marked;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1375	  CAMLassert(pb_size(&pb) == 0);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1376	  return budget;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1377	}

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1379	/* mark until the budget runs out or marking is done */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1380	static intnat mark(intnat budget) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1381	  caml_domain_state *domain_state = Caml_state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1382	  while (budget > 0 && !domain_state->marking_done) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1383	    budget = do_some_marking(domain_state->mark_stack, budget);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1384	    if (budget > 0) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1385	      struct mark_stack* mstk = domain_state->mark_stack;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1386	      addrmap_iterator it = mstk->compressed_stack_iter;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1387	      if (caml_addrmap_iter_ok(&mstk->compressed_stack, it)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1388	        uintnat chunk = caml_addrmap_iter_key(&mstk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1389	        uintnat bitset = caml_addrmap_iter_value(&mstk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1390	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1391	        /* NB: must update the iterator here, as possible that
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1392	           mark_slice_darken could lead to the mark stack being pruned
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1393	           and invalidation of the iterator */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1394	        mstk->compressed_stack_iter =
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1395	                      caml_addrmap_next(&mstk->compressed_stack, it);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1396	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1397	        for(int ofs=0; ofs<BITS_PER_WORD; ofs++) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1398	          if(bitset & ((uintnat)1 << ofs)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1399	            value_ptr p = chunk_and_offset_to_ptr(chunk, ofs);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1400	            mark_slice_darken(domain_state->mark_stack, *p, &budget);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1401	          }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1402	        }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1403	      } else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1404	        ephe_next_cycle ();
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1405	        domain_state->marking_done = 1;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1406	        (void)caml_atomic_counter_decr(&num_domains_to_mark);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1407	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1408	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1409	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1410	  return budget;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1411	}

[tool-e33] read_file ->   1413	static scanning_action_flags darken_scanning_flags = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1414	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1415	void caml_darken_cont(value cont)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1416	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1417	  CAMLassert(Is_block(cont));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1418	  CAMLassert(!Is_young(cont));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1419	  CAMLassert(Tag_val(cont) == Cont_tag);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1420	  {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1421	    SPIN_WAIT {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1422	      header_t hd = atomic_load_relaxed(Hp_atomic_val(cont));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1423	      CAMLassert(!Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.GARBAGE));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1424	      if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1425	        /* Perform an acquire load to synchronize with the marking domain */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1426	        hd = atomic_load_acquire(Hp_atomic_val(cont));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1427	        if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED))
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1428	          break;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1429	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1430	      if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.UNMARKED) &&
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1431	          atomic_compare_exchange_strong(
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1432	              Hp_atomic_val(cont), &hd,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1433	              With_status_hd(hd, NOT_MARKABLE))) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1434	        value stk = Field(cont, 0);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1435	        if (Ptr_val(stk) != NULL)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1436	          caml_scan_stack(&caml_darken, darken_scanning_flags, Caml_state,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1437	                          Ptr_val(stk), 0);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1438	        atomic_store_release(Hp_atomic_val(cont),
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1439	                             With_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1440	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1441	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1442	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1443	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1444	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1445	void caml_darken(void* state, value v, volatile value* ignored) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1446	  header_t hd;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1447	  if (!Is_markable (v)) return; /* foreign stack, at least */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1448	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1449	  hd = Hd_val(v);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1450	  if (Tag_hd(hd) == Infix_tag) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1451	    v -= Infix_offset_hd(hd);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1452	    hd = Hd_val(v);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1453	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1454	  if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.UNMARKED)) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1455	    caml_domain_state* domain_state = (caml_domain_state*)state;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1456	    if (domain_state->marking_done) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1457	      (void)caml_atomic_counter_incr(&num_domains_to_mark);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1458	      domain_state->marking_done = 0;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1459	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1460	    if (Tag_hd(hd) == Cont_tag) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1461	      caml_darken_cont(v);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1462	    } else {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1463	      atomic_store_relaxed(
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1464	         Hp_atomic_val(v),
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1465	         With_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.MARKED));
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1466	      if (Tag_hd(hd) < No_scan_tag) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1467	        mark_stack_push_block(domain_state->mark_stack, v);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1468	      }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1469	    }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1470	  }
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1471	}
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1472	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1473	/*******************************************************************************
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1474	 * Major GC cycle
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1475	 ******************************************************************************/
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1476	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1477	static void cycle_major_heap_from_stw_single(
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1478	  caml_domain_state* domain,
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1479	  uintnat num_domains_in_stw)
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1480	{
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1481	  /* Cycle major heap */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1482	  /* FIXME: delete caml_cycle_heap_from_stw_single
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1483	     and have per-domain copies of the data? */
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1484	  caml_cycle_heap_from_stw_single();
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1485	  caml_gc_log("GC cycle %" CAML_PRIuNAT " completed (heap cycled)",
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1486	              caml_major_cycles_completed);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1487	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1488	  caml_major_cycles_completed++;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1489	  CAML_GC_MESSAGE(SLICESIZE, "Starting major GC cycle\n");
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1490	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1491	  if (atomic_load_relaxed(&caml_verb_gc) & CAML_GC_MSG_STATS) {
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1492	    struct gc_stats s;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1493	    intnat heap_words, not_garbage_words, swept_words;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1494	
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1495	    caml_compute_gc_stats(&s);
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1496	    heap_words = s.heap_stats.pool_words + s.heap_stats.large_words;
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1497	    not_garbage_words = s.heap_stats.pool_live_words
[tool-e33] read_file ->   1498	      + s.heap_stats.large_words;

[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2038	void caml_major_collection_slice(intnat howmuch)
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2039	{
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2040	  uintnat major_slice_epoch = atomic_load (&caml_major_slice_epoch);
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2041	
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2042	  /* if this is an auto-triggered GC slice, make it interruptible */
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2043	  if (howmuch == AUTO_TRIGGERED_MAJOR_SLICE) {
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2044	    major_collection_slice(
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2045	        AUTO_TRIGGERED_MAJOR_SLICE,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2046	        0,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2047	        0,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2048	        Slice_interruptible,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2049	        0
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2050	        );

[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2052	      caml_gc_log("Major slice interrupted, rescheduling major slice");
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2053	      caml_request_major_slice(0);
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2054	    }
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2055	  } else {
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2056	    /* TODO: could make forced API slices interruptible, but would need to do
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2057	       accounting or pass up interrupt */
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2058	    major_collection_slice(howmuch, 0, 0, Slice_uninterruptible, 0);
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2059	  }

[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2061	   */

[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2063	}

[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2074	static void stw_finish_major_cycle (caml_domain_state* domain, void* arg,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2075	                                         int participating_count,
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2076	                                         caml_domain_state** participating)
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2077	{
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2078	  /* We must copy params because the leader may exit this
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2079	    before other domains do. There is at least one barrier somewhere
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2080	    in the major cycle ending, so we don't need one immediately
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2081	    after this. */
[tool-5d0] read_file ->   2082	  struct finish_major_cycle_params params =

[tool-97b] read_file ->    287	static void calc_pool_stats(pool* a, sizeclass sz, struct heap_stats* s)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    288	{
[tool-97b] read_file ->    289	  header_t* p = POOL_FIRST_BLOCK(a, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    290	  header_t* end = POOL_END(a);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    291	  mlsize_t wh = wsize_sizeclass[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    292	  s->pool_frag_words += POOL_SLAB_WOFFSET(sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    293	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    294	  while (p + wh <= end) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    295	    header_t hd = (header_t)atomic_load_relaxed((atomic_uintnat*)p);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    296	    if (!POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HD(hd)) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    297	      s->pool_live_words += Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    298	      s->pool_frag_words += wh - Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    299	      s->pool_live_blocks++;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    300	    } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    301	      p += wh * Wosize_hd(hd); /* skip contiguous free blocks */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    302	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    303	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    304	    p += wh;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    305	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    306	  CAMLassert(end == p);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    307	  s->pool_words += POOL_WSIZE;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    308	}

[tool-97b] read_file ->    342	/* Adopt pool from the pool_freelist avail and full pools
[tool-97b] read_file ->    343	   to satisfy an allocation */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    344	static pool* pool_global_adopt(struct caml_heap_state* local, sizeclass sz)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    345	{
[tool-97b] read_file ->    346	  pool* r = NULL;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    347	  int adopted_pool = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    348	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    349	  /* probably no available pools out there to be had */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    350	  if( !atomic_load_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_avail_pools[sz]) &&
[tool-97b] read_file ->    351	      !atomic_load_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_full_pools[sz]) )
[tool-97b] read_file ->    352	    return NULL;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    353	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    354	  /* Haven't managed to find a pool locally, try the global ones */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    355	  caml_plat_lock_blocking(&pool_freelist.lock);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    356	  if( atomic_load_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_avail_pools[sz]) ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    357	    r = atomic_load_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_avail_pools[sz]);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    358	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    359	    if( r ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    360	      atomic_store_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_avail_pools[sz], r->next);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    361	      r->next = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    362	      r->owner = local->owner;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    363	      local->avail_pools[sz] = r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    364	      adopt_pool_stats_with_lock(local, r, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    365	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    366	      #ifdef DEBUG
[tool-97b] read_file ->    367	      {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    368	        value* next_obj = r->next_obj;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    369	        while( next_obj ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    370	          CAMLassert(POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(next_obj));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    371	          next_obj = (value*)next_obj[1];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    372	        }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    373	      }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    374	      #endif
[tool-97b] read_file ->    375	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    376	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    377	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    378	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    379	  /* There were no global avail pools, so let's adopt one of the full ones and
[tool-97b] read_file ->    380	     try our luck sweeping it later on */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    381	  if( !r ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    382	    r = atomic_load_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_full_pools[sz]);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    383	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    384	    if( r ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    385	      atomic_store_relaxed(&pool_freelist.global_full_pools[sz], r->next);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    386	      r->next = local->full_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    387	      r->owner = local->owner;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    388	      local->full_pools[sz] = r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    389	      adopt_pool_stats_with_lock(local, r, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    390	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    391	      adopted_pool = 1;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    392	      r = 0; // this pool is full
[tool-97b] read_file ->    393	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    394	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    395	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    396	  caml_plat_unlock(&pool_freelist.lock);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    397	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    398	  if( !r && adopted_pool ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    399	    Caml_state->major_work_done_between_slices +=
[tool-97b] read_file ->    400	      pool_sweep(local, &local->full_pools[sz], sz, 0);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    401	    r = local->avail_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    402	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    403	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    404	  CAMLassert(r == NULL || r->owner == local->owner);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    405	  return r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    406	}
[tool-97b] read_file ->    407	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    408	/* Allocating an object from a pool */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    409	static pool* pool_find(struct caml_heap_state* local, sizeclass sz) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    410	  pool* r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    411	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    412	  /* Hopefully we have a pool we can use directly */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    413	  r = local->avail_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    414	  if (r) return r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    415	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    416	  /* Otherwise, try to sweep until we find one */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    417	  while (!local->avail_pools[sz] && local->unswept_avail_pools[sz]) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    418	    Caml_state->major_work_done_between_slices +=
[tool-97b] read_file ->    419	      pool_sweep(local, &local->unswept_avail_pools[sz], sz, 0);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    420	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    421	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    422	  r = local->avail_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    423	  if (r) return r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    424	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    425	  /* Haven't managed to find a pool locally, try the global ones */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    426	  r = pool_global_adopt(local, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    427	  if (r) return r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    428	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    429	  /* Failing that, we need to allocate a new pool */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    430	  r = pool_acquire(local);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    431	  if (!r) return 0; /* if we can't allocate, give up */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    432	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    433	  local->stats.pool_words += POOL_WSIZE;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    434	  if (local->stats.pool_words > local->stats.pool_max_words)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    435	    local->stats.pool_max_words = local->stats.pool_words;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    436	  local->stats.pool_frag_words += POOL_HEADER_WSIZE + wastage_sizeclass[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    437	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    438	  /* Having allocated a new pool, set it up for size sz */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    439	  local->avail_pools[sz] = r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    440	  pool_initialize(r, sz, local->owner);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    441	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    442	  return r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    443	}
[tool-97b] read_file ->    444	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    445	static void* pool_allocate(struct caml_heap_state* local, sizeclass sz) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    446	  value* p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    447	  value* next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    448	  pool* r = pool_find(local, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    449	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    450	  if (!r) return 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    451	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    452	  p = r->next_obj;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    453	  /* assert that p is inside the pool */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    454	  CAMLassert(p >= (value*)POOL_FIRST_BLOCK(r, sz));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    455	  CAMLassert(p < (value*)r + POOL_WSIZE);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    456	  CAMLassert(POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(p));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    457	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    458	  /* in this case there are more free blocks immediately after */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    459	  if( Wosize_hp(p) > 0 ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    460	    next = (value*)(p + wsize_sizeclass[sz]);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    461	    /* we update the pool header of the next block */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    462	    *next = POOL_FREE_HEADER(Wosize_hp(p) - 1);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    463	    /* also copy the next_obj pointer from p */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    464	    CAMLassert(p[1] == 0 || POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(p[1]));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    465	    next[1] = p[1];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    466	  } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    467	    next = (value*)p[1];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    468	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    469	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    470	  r->next_obj = next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    471	  if (!next) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    472	    local->avail_pools[sz] = r->next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    473	    r->next = local->full_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    474	    local->full_pools[sz] = r;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    475	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    476	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    477	  CAMLassert(
[tool-97b] read_file ->    478	    /* either there's no more free space and we've moved the pool */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    479	    (r->next_obj == 0 && local->full_pools[sz] == r)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    480	    /* or there's still free space */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    481	    || POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(r->next_obj));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    482	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    483	  return p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    484	}

[tool-97b] read_file ->    486	static void* large_allocate(struct caml_heap_state* local, mlsize_t sz) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    487	  large_alloc* a = malloc(sz + LARGE_ALLOC_HEADER_SZ);

[tool-97b] read_file ->    490	  if (local->stats.large_words > local->stats.large_max_words)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    491	    local->stats.large_max_words = local->stats.large_words;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    492	  local->stats.large_blocks++;

[tool-97b] read_file ->    499	value* caml_shared_try_alloc(struct caml_heap_state* local, mlsize_t wosize,

[tool-97b] read_file ->    542	/* Sweeping of the major heap shared pools */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    543	static intnat pool_sweep(struct caml_heap_state* local, pool** plist,
[tool-97b] read_file ->    544	                         sizeclass sz, int release_to_global_pool) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    545	  intnat work;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    546	  pool* a = *plist;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    547	  if (!a) return 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    548	  *plist = a->next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    549	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    550	  {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    551	    header_t* p = POOL_FIRST_BLOCK(a, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    552	    header_t* last_free_block = NULL;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    553	    const header_t* end = POOL_END(a);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    554	    const mlsize_t wh = wsize_sizeclass[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    555	    int all_used = 1;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    556	    struct heap_stats* s = &local->stats;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    557	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    558	    CAMLassert(a->owner == local->owner);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    559	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    560	    a->next_obj = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    561	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    562	    /* note that the below will have to be changed for the new GC pacing
[tool-97b] read_file ->    563	      logic */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    564	    work = end - p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    565	    do {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    566	      header_t hd = (header_t)atomic_load_relaxed((atomic_uintnat*)p);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    567	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    568	      if( (char*)p + caml_plat_pagesize < (char*)end ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    569	        caml_prefetch((char*)p + caml_plat_pagesize);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    570	      }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    571	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    572	      /* The pools mark a block as being free by setting the tag to No_scan_tag
[tool-97b] read_file ->    573	        and the color to NOT_MARKABLE. The wosize is used to indicate the
[tool-97b] read_file ->    574	        number of contiguous free blocks that follow. The first field is a
[tool-97b] read_file ->    575	        pointer to the next free block beyond the immediately following
[tool-97b] read_file ->    576	        contiguous free blocks (if any). */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    577	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    578	      /* Check if the current block is garbage, if it is turn it into a free
[tool-97b] read_file ->    579	      block */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    580	      if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.GARBAGE)) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    581	        CAMLassert(Whsize_hd(hd) <= wh);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    582	        if (Tag_hd (hd) == Custom_tag) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    583	          void (*final_fun)(value) = Custom_ops_val(Val_hp(p))->finalize;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    584	          if (final_fun != NULL) final_fun(Val_hp(p));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    585	        }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    586	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    587	        /* add to freelist. This could be optimised, we don't need
[tool-97b] read_file ->    588	        to write the free header if we're going to merge it with a prior
[tool-97b] read_file ->    589	        free block but it makes this codepath more complex. */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    590	        *p = POOL_FREE_HEADER(0);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    591	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    592	        CAMLassert(Is_block((value)p));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    593	#ifdef DEBUG
[tool-97b] read_file ->    594	        for (mlsize_t i = 1, wo = Wosize_whsize(wh); i < wo; i++) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    595	          Field(Val_hp(p), i) = Debug_free_major;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    596	        }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    597	#endif
[tool-97b] read_file ->    598	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    599	        all_used = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    600	        /* update stats */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    601	        s->pool_live_blocks--;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    602	        s->pool_live_words -= Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    603	        local->owner->swept_words += Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    604	        s->pool_frag_words -= (wh - Whsize_hd(hd));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    605	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    606	        /* reload hd */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    607	        hd = POOL_FREE_HEADER(0);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    608	      }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    609	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    610	      /* If the current block was garbage (and is now a free block) or was
[tool-97b] read_file ->    611	      initially a free block, see if we can merge it with the last free block we
[tool-97b] read_file ->    612	      encountered or if we cannot then update the pointer in the last free block
[tool-97b] read_file ->    613	      to point to this one */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    614	      if (POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HD(hd)) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    615	        /* if any block is free then this is no longer a full pool */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    616	        all_used = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    617	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    618	        /* if there was a free block before us, check first if we can
[tool-97b] read_file ->    619	           merge with it */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    620	        if( last_free_block ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    621	          CAMLassert(POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(last_free_block));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    622	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    623	          /* check if we can merge with the last free block */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    624	          if( last_free_block + (1 + Wosize_hp(last_free_block)) * wh == p ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    625	            /* if we can then update the wosize of the last free block */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    626	            *last_free_block = POOL_FREE_HEADER(Wosize_hp(last_free_block)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    627	                                                  + Wosize_hd(hd) + 1);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    628	          } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    629	            /* in this case there's a non-free block between us so update
[tool-97b] read_file ->    630	                the next pointer */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    631	            last_free_block[1] = (value)p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    632	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    633	            last_free_block = p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    634	          }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    635	        } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    636	          /* if we're the first free block then set the next_obj pointer for
[tool-97b] read_file ->    637	            the pool (which indicates the start of the freelist) */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    638	          a->next_obj = (value*)p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    639	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    640	          last_free_block = p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    641	        }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    642	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    643	        /* add the free blocks following this block, skipping over them */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    644	        p += wh * Wosize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    645	      } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    646	        /* there's still a live block, the pool can't be released to the global
[tool-97b] read_file ->    647	            freelist */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    648	        release_to_global_pool = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    649	      }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    650	      p += Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    651	    } while (p + wh <= end);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    652	    CAMLassert(p == end);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    653	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    654	    if( !all_used ) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    655	      /* the last free block should have 0 as its next pointer */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    656	      last_free_block[1] = 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    657	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    658	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    659	    CAMLassert(
[tool-97b] read_file ->    660	      /* if all spaces are used then next_obj should be 0 */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    661	      (all_used && !a->next_obj)
[tool-97b] read_file ->    662	      /* otherwise it should point to a free block */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    663	      || (!all_used && POOL_BLOCK_FREE_HP(a->next_obj))
[tool-97b] read_file ->    664	    );
[tool-97b] read_file ->    665	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    666	    if (release_to_global_pool) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    667	      pool_release(local, a, sz);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    668	    } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    669	      pool** list = all_used ? &local->full_pools[sz] : &local->avail_pools[sz];
[tool-97b] read_file ->    670	      a->next = *list;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    671	      *list = a;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    672	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    673	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    674	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    675	  return work;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    676	}
[tool-97b] read_file ->    677	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    678	static intnat large_alloc_sweep(struct caml_heap_state* local) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    679	  value* p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    680	  header_t hd;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    681	  large_alloc* a = local->unswept_large;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    682	  if (!a) return 0;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    683	  local->unswept_large = a->next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    684	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    685	  p = (value*)((char*)a + LARGE_ALLOC_HEADER_SZ);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    686	  /* The header being read here may be concurrently written by a thread doing
[tool-97b] read_file ->    687	     marking. This is fine because marking can only make UNMARKED objects
[tool-97b] read_file ->    688	     MARKED or NOT_MARKABLE, all of which are treated identically here. */
[tool-97b] read_file ->    689	  hd = Hd_hp(p);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    690	  if (Has_status_hd(hd, caml_global_heap_state.GARBAGE)) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    691	    if (Tag_hd (hd) == Custom_tag) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    692	      void (*final_fun)(value) = Custom_ops_val(Val_hp(p))->finalize;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    693	      if (final_fun != NULL) final_fun(Val_hp(p));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    694	    }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    695	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    696	    local->stats.large_words -=
[tool-97b] read_file ->    697	      Whsize_hd(hd) + Wsize_bsize(LARGE_ALLOC_HEADER_SZ);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    698	    local->owner->swept_words +=
[tool-97b] read_file ->    699	      Whsize_hd(hd) + Wsize_bsize(LARGE_ALLOC_HEADER_SZ);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    700	    local->stats.large_blocks--;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    701	    free(a);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    702	  } else {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    703	    a->next = local->swept_large;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    704	    local->swept_large = a;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    705	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    706	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    707	  return Whsize_hd(hd);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    708	}
[tool-97b] read_file ->    709	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    710	static void large_alloc_finalise(struct caml_heap_state* local) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    711	  value* p;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    712	  header_t hd;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    713	  large_alloc* a;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    714

[tool-97b] read_file ->    716	    local->unswept_large = a->next;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    717	
[tool-97b] read_file ->    718	    p = (value*)((char*)a + LARGE_ALLOC_HEADER_SZ);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    719	    hd = Hd_hp(p);
[tool-97b] read_file ->    720	    if (Tag_hd (hd) == Custom_tag) {
[tool-97b] read_file ->    721	      void (*final_fun)(value) = Custom_ops_val(Val_hp(p))->finalize;
[tool-97b] read_file ->    722	      if (final_fun != NULL) final_fun(Val_hp(p));
[tool-97b] read_file ->    723	    }

[tool-97b] read_file ->    725	  }
[tool-97b] read_file ->    726	}
